# Spec revision 59: what changed in the records

Generated by `tools/record_changes.py`; do not edit. Each generated record as committed at
`7c0b63f` (2026-11-12: the records of revision 57's regeneration, its emergence audit and its
stage-end sweep, which revisions 58 and 59 left as they were), against the record as committed now, after
revision 59's one regeneration (T-001 step 4). Revision 59 is the engine batch of the whole-repo review of
2026-11-11 (`docs/plan_engine_batch_2026-11-12.md`): it changed rules (among them `M5.D.3`, `M5.D.6`,
`M5.D.9`, `M5.C.1`, `M6.4`) and the verdict's own fields (`M11.1f`: each arm's own required moves, every
count an arm records, each readout's share of differing pairs), and added two mutants (T-237). A changed
verdict is therefore a **new result under new rules**, reported beside revision 57's and version 1's verdicts
and never in their place (`M11.F`); a premise passing is never a finding (`M11.5`). It is not a
defect. A row's verdict is its outcome (a criterion, or a criterion under a sweep setting) or whether the
criterion caught the mutant (a mutation row). An effect **moved** when its mean difference changed by more than
the half-width revision 57's record stated; moved effects are listed only for rows whose verdict held. "Differ at
all" counts every field of a row on both sides that is not exactly revision 57's (the verdict, the seed count, the
flags, each readout's mean difference, half-width and p), including a field on one side only; each is listed
under its record, and "No change" means none differs. A record's hash line must differ from revision 57's, or the
tool refuses: an unchanged hash means it was not regenerated. A record with no row on both sides is refused too.

| Record | Hash at revision 57 | Hash now | Rows compared | Verdicts changed | Effects moved | Differ at all | Only at revision 57 | Only now |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `docs/phase_c_ensemble_record.md` | `b751f1941dfe` | `0aa43ef6868a` | 30 | 0 | 1 | 400 | 0 | 0 |
| `docs/phase_d_ensemble_record.md` | `b751f1941dfe` | `0aa43ef6868a` | 25 | 1 | 0 | 220 | 0 | 0 |
| `docs/phase_d2_ensemble_record.md` | `b751f1941dfe` | `0aa43ef6868a` | 9 | 1 | 0 | 195 | 0 | 0 |

In the level-occupancy record, which has no verdicts, "Verdicts changed" counts the rows whose values changed.

**Not compared here:**

- `docs/phase_c_diagnostic_record.md`: it has no machine-readable block; `git diff 7c0b63f -- docs/phase_c_diagnostic_record.md` shows what changed.
- `docs/review/phase_c_trace_seed7.md, docs/review/phase_c_chart_seed7.html`: one seed's run, rendered; S1.7a adds lock-in release rows by design, so `git diff 7c0b63f` on them shows that and any change of trajectory.
- `docs/review/phase_b_trace_seed7.md, docs/review/phase_b_trace_seed7_nadia.md`: Phase B's seed-7 traces, rendered at Phase B's close (`3132c69`) and not regenerated since, so they are revision 57's.
- `docs/phase_c_completion_report.md §11`: generated from the Phase C records compared here (`tools/c16_report.py`).
- `docs/spec_coverage.md`: generated from the tests and the ensemble and mutation records compared here.
- `docs/STATUS.md`: generated by `tools/status.py` from the spec, its index, the revision log and the records compared here (T-225, 2026-11-11); it holds no result of its own.
- `docs/phase_b_mutation_record.md`: the record of Phase B's close at commit `3132c69`, frozen (plan D5) and not regenerated, so it is revision 57's apart from the status line stage 1 added; `tools/mutation_gate.py` refuses to rewrite it.
- `docs/version_2_sweep_record.md, docs/version_2_status.md`: the status page is generated from the records compared here (`tools/version_2_status.py`); the sweep record is rendered from its own cache (`tools/version_2_sweep.py`) and was rerun on revision 59's engine by the stage-end sweep, split across both Macs, so its results are not revision 57's; `git diff 7c0b63f` on it shows each criterion's held fraction then and now.
- `docs/emergence_audit_record.md`: the emergence audit (`M11.1g`), rendered from its own cache (`tools/emergence_audit.py`) and rerun on revision 59's engine by this regeneration (T-001 step 4); it has no machine-readable verdicts this tool compares, so `git diff 7c0b63f` on it shows what moved.
- `docs/review/group_g_readout.md`: revision 36's before-and-after measurement on 20 seeds (`tools/group_g_readout.py`): its "after" section is regenerated on each later engine, so `git diff 7c0b63f` on it shows what revision 59 moved.
- `docs/review/stage_3_projection_trace.md, docs/review/stage_3_iposition_probe.md, docs/review/stage_3_t130_event_sizes.md, docs/review/stage_3_displaced_coping_probe.md`: stage 3's measurements before any rule changed, kept as measured on their steps' engines and not regenerated.
- `docs/review/stage_3_unit_mutation_gate.md, docs/review/stage_3_step_7_criteria.md, docs/review/stage_3_step_7_c2_mutants.md, docs/review/stage_3_step_8_criteria.md, docs/review/stage_3_step_9_criteria.md`: stage 3's measurements between the open batch's commits (D31), kept as measured on their steps' engines and not regenerated.
- `docs/review/social_cues_unit_mutation_gate.md`: spec revision 55's unit mutation proofs for the social cues (`tools/mutation_gate.py`, T-202), measured before the regeneration and new at revision 55; nothing at revision 57 to compare.
- `docs/version_1_status.md`: version 1's status page, frozen at the tag: `tests/test_version_1_status.py` checks that it is the tag's, byte for byte.
- `docs/review/phase_c_famdiag_seed7.json`: the seed-7 run as a family diagram (`tools/famdiag_export.py`); regenerated by revision 59's regeneration (`tools/famdiag_export.py --seed 7`), so `git diff 7c0b63f` on it shows any change of trajectory under revision 59's rules.
- `docs/review/t176_negative_run_trace.md`: T-176's trace of `M11.C.48` (`tools/t176_negative_run_trace.py`, in no record's hash), rerun on revision 59's engine, so `git diff 7c0b63f` on it shows what the engine batch moved.
- `docs/review/2026-11-09_sc_pass_probes.md, docs/review/sc_pass_probes/c52_recorded.jsonl, docs/review/sc_pass_probes/c52_recorded_mutant.jsonl, docs/review/sc_pass_probes/c52_equal.jsonl, docs/review/sc_pass_probes/c52_equal_mutant.jsonl, docs/review/sc_pass_probes/c52_off.jsonl, docs/review/sc_pass_probes/c55_recorded.jsonl, docs/review/sc_pass_probes/c55_off.jsonl`: T-206's and T-208's probes of `M11.C.52` and `M11.C.55`, after revision 55, and their per-seed rows (`tools/sc_pass_probe.py`, in no record's hash); nothing at revision 57 to compare.
- `docs/review/2026-11-10_revisions_51_55_review_prep.md`: the review prep for spec revisions 51 to 55, written from the documents and the committed records and deciding nothing (2026-11-10); it runs nothing, so there is no result of its own to compare.
